- **opscale-graph**: Closes the Plan phase: builds the module's traceability graph from the Plan JSONs — graph.json (a nested {id, ref} hierarchy, each ref-ing the phase that owns it) + graph.html (an interactive, self-contained card tree). Step 5 of Plan, after opscale-sipoc, before Generate; reads docs/plan/*.json. Replaces the retired opscale-ontology. Runs before opscale-domain; the Plan phase still runs without it. Trigger: "start a new module", "initialize the project", "configure tests and debug".
